# E1-T10 · E1 integration and gate evidence (backend)

## Objective
Prove the whole identity vertical end to end against the local stack (and staging when E0-T13 exists) so the organizer can run the E1 gate with Jordi.

## Context to load
`roadmap/ROADMAP.md` (gate E1 checklist) · the identity code · this file.

## Steps
1. `bin/e1-smoke` (bash + curl + jq, or python): magic link for a seeded member at the Cànic host (mailbox = `LogEmailSender` output or the SendGrid test mailbox) → token grant → `/me` → `PUT /me/profile` → refresh rotation → reuse rejected → password set → login by password → lockout after 5 failures → impersonation by an admin → handoff to `clubs-admin` → OIDC code+PKCE with the test client → revoke → `import-learn --dry-run` with the fixture.
2. Run it locally and paste the full output; if staging exists (E0-T13), run it there too.
3. Update `README.md` «Auth for local development» and `docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md`.
4. **Locale set (organizer 06-09)**: widen the identity locale contract from `ca/es/en` to the seven product locales of ADR-011/S01 §14 (`ca es en fr de no pt`) in `MeAccount.locale`, `PATCH /me`, `OnboardingFields.locale`, `PlatformAccountRequest.locale` and the `AccountService` validator (regression T-01-23 updated); UI languages stay `ca/es/en` (others fall back to `en` in the apps). Snapshot regenerated.

## Verification (run all; paste full output in the report)
- `bin/e1-smoke` exit 0 with every step printed.
- `./mvnw -q verify` green; JaCoCo thresholds hold.

## Definition of Done
Smoke script committed and green; gate checklist items marked in the report.

## Evidence to return
Smoke output; coverage summary.

---

## Executor report
Implemented E1-T10 on 2026-09-09. Local smoke and full Maven verification pass: 204 unit/contract tests and 344 integration tests, zero failures/errors/skips; all configured JaCoCo thresholds hold. All declared dependencies were verified at task selection. No other roadmap task was taken.

### Branch / commits
Working-tree edits only. No git write commands; the session publish script owns commit/push on main.

### Evidence (full command outputs)
Full stdout/stderr is reproduced below. Hex hashes/opaque identifiers are truncated as required by AGENTS.md; trailing log whitespace is normalized, and no command output text is otherwise omitted. Maven uses the existing writable temporary wrapper/dependency cache. The failed smoke attempts record harness corrections; final smoke run 5 and verify run 2 are green. The first verify failure was the remaining obsolete French-rejection assertion. Smoke run 3 overlapped Maven repackaging and its CLI failure was not diagnosed beyond its exit status; subsequent runs were serialized. Smoke run 4 showed outbox dispatch changing the comparison snapshot, resolved by disabling scheduling in the disposable fixture.

Additional checks: `git diff --check`, `python3 -m py_compile bin/e1-smoke`, `docker ps --filter name=e1-smoke- --format '{{.Names}}'`. Each exited 0 with no stdout/stderr; no smoke containers remain.

### Files changed
- `bin/e1-smoke`: executable Python rehearsal; disposable MongoDB 7 replica set and local API, seed-based hosts/accounts, private mailbox, cookie grants, negative checks, Learn preview and cleanup.
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/{MeResponse,IdentityRequests,IdentityResponses}.java` and `identity/application/AccountService.java`: seven product locale values, including the AccountSummary response.
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/application/LearnImportService.java`: interpret offset-free export timestamps in Europe/Madrid; preserve explicit offsets.
- `src/main/java/com/agilityhub/core/clubs/messaging/application/LogEmailSender.java`, `configuration/EmailConfiguration.java`, `src/main/resources/application.yml`, `.env.example`: opt-in local mailbox via MAIL_LOCAL_DIRECTORY; atomic JSON files, 0700 directory and 0600 files; no message contents in diagnostic logs.
- `src/test/java/com/agilityhub/core/identity/api/{IdentityCoreIT,OnboardingIT,LearnImportIT}.java`: locale persistence/refreshed claims, onboarding locales and import winter/summer/explicit-offset regressions; replace obsolete French-rejection assertions with unsupported `xx`.
- `src/test/java/com/agilityhub/core/configuration/{EmailConfigurationTest,OpenApiSnapshotTest}.java`: private mailbox success/failure/profile isolation and all five identity locale schema enums.
- `README.md`, `docs/openapi/CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/openapi/openapi.json`, `CHANGELOG.md`: local auth/cookie/cleanup instructions, import timestamp semantics and regenerated snapshot.
- `roadmap/tasks/E1-T10.md`, `roadmap/MESSAGES.md`; STATUS.md rendered only through check.py.

### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)
- T-01-23: all seven locales persist through PATCH /me and onboarding, reach refreshed JWT claims, and appear in MeAccount/AccountSummary/AccountPatchRequest/OnboardingFields/PlatformAccountRequest schemas. Unsupported `xx` still raises LOCALE_NOT_SUPPORTED.
- T-01-14 / R-01-12: Learn winter and summer wall-clock conversion, explicit Z/+03:00 preservation, existing 50-account bcrypt/idempotency coverage, and CLI dry-run document equality.
- T-01-08: opt-in local mailbox persists complete messages atomically with private permissions; failures report no delivery, clean pending files and expose no message contents; test profile remains FakeEmailSender.
- Smoke exercises R-01-04 (magic links), R-01-06 (rotation/reuse), R-01-07 (profile), R-01-09 (impersonation), R-01-10 (revoke), R-01-11 (OIDC), R-01-13 (handoff), password setting and lockout after five failures. Existing tenant/role tests run in full verification; no new HTTP endpoints.

#### E1 gate evidence for organizer review
ROADMAP.md contains an E1 overview but no explicit Gate E1 checklist. The following checklist maps this task's Steps and records the remaining external items without changing ROADMAP.md:
- [x] Local Mongo replica set and API health UP with fictional Cànic/minimal seeds.
- [x] Magic-link mailbox → grant → /me → persisted profile → cookie rotation → reuse and revoked-family rejection.
- [x] First password → successful password login → fifth failure returns 429 LOGIN_LOCKED with Retry-After; correct password remains denied while locked.
- [x] Admin impersonation → member bootstrap → sensitive write denied → impersonation revoked.
- [x] Handoff to seeded clubs-admin host → ADMIN bootstrap → replay denied.
- [x] OIDC discovery → S256 required → fresh global login + flow cookie → code exchange → ID claims/userinfo → replay denied.
- [x] OIDC and handoff sessions revoked; subsequent refresh rejected.
- [x] Learn fixture dry-run: 50 proposed accounts, no identity/audit/outbox mutation.
- [x] Seven identity account locales and regenerated OpenAPI snapshot.
- [x] Full verify: 204 unit/contract + 344 integration tests pass; identity application 97.63% lines / 89.98% branches, identity domain 100% / 100%, identity API 97.97% lines. All configured package gates pass.
- [ ] Staging/real SendGrid delivery: E0-T13 is blocked; local LogEmailSender mailbox only.
- [ ] Real Learn adapter/federation: E1-T08 is not open; not exercised. Existing provisioning/password-sync endpoint contracts still return 501.
- [ ] CI after session publication and organizer/Jordi gate approval.

### Assumptions
- The smoke owns a fresh local database/container/API. Seed-derived fictional hosts/accounts are used; a passwordless account and minimal tenant member projection are prepared only in that disposable database because census creation is not implemented yet. No existing developer database or seed file is modified.
- The current cookie delivery contract governs all three browser clients. A second magic link creates a new session after intentional refresh reuse invalidates the first family. OIDC uses the existing public id-web client with a local test issuer/callback override and simulates its HTTPS Host/cookie over the loopback API socket.
- LogEmailSender intentionally logs no credentials. MAIL_LOCAL_DIRECTORY adds an opt-in local mailbox, ignored by production/SendGrid/test senders; no SMTP/SendGrid message is sent. POSIX file permissions target the documented macOS/Linux development environments.
- Background scheduling is disabled in the disposable fixture so the dry-run snapshot cannot race outbox dispatch. Authentication rate limits and HIBP remain enabled, including A2's existing availability fallback. The smoke is run after packaging completes.
- Organizer's 2026-09-09 answer supersedes the prior UTC import assumption. Java resolves ambiguous autumn wall times with the earlier offset and moves nonexistent spring times forward; explicit offsets disambiguate transition hours. No data-model/spec contradiction was newly introduced.
- UI translation work remains in the apps: account locale support is seven languages, translated UI remains ca/es/en with English fallback. AccountSummary is widened with the request to avoid a contradictory response schema; provisioning implementation remains outside this task.

### Questions / catalog proposals
No new catalog parameters, domain events, notification types, audit actions or ErrorCodes. The local mailbox setting is deployment configuration, not a business parameter. No access question blocks this local task. Organizer gate limits and the Learn timestamp interpretation are recorded in MESSAGES.md; staging/real delivery, current 501 Learn provisioning/password-sync routes, the unopened adapter task and post-publication CI remain explicit gate follow-ups.
